/// Logic
component FengGameManagerMKII
{
    function Init()
    {
        self.randomTeleporterManager = RandomLinkedTeleporterManager();
    }

    function OnGameStart()
    {
        self.randomTeleporterManager.OnGameStart();
    }

    function OnResetRace()
    {
        self.randomTeleporterManager.OnResetRace();
    }
}

class RandomLinkedTeleporterManager
{
    # MapObject lists of the reserved 'Start' and 'End' teleporter labels.
    _startTeleporters = null;
    _endTeleporters = null;
    # Dictionary of non-reserved teleporter group labels mapped to lists of teleporter MapObjects.
    _teleporterDict = null;
    # String list of group labels included in the random sequence.
    _randomSequenceLabels = null;

    function OnGameStart()
    {
        self.Setup();
    }

    function Setup()
    {
        self.FindTeleporters();
        self.InitTeleporters();
        self.InitRandomTeleporters();
    }

    function OnResetRace()
    {
        self._startTeleporters = null;
        self._endTeleporters = null;
        self._randomSequenceLabels = null;
        
        allTeleporters = Map.FindMapObjectsByComponent("LinkedTeleporter");
        for (teleporter in allTeleporters)
        {
            teleporterComponent = teleporter.GetComponent("LinkedTeleporter");
            teleporterComponent._initialized = false;
            teleporterComponent._targetPosition = null;
            teleporterComponent._targetComponent = null;
            teleporterComponent._enterVector = null;
            teleporterComponent._exitVector = null;
            teleporterComponent._exitSet = false;
            teleporterComponent._teleportCooldown = false;
            teleporterComponent.OnGameStart();
        }

        self.Setup();
    }

    # Find all teleporter objects and store them based on type.
    function FindTeleporters()
    {
        self._startTeleporters = List();
        self._endTeleporters = List();
        self._teleporterDict = Dict();
        self._randomSequenceLabels = List();
        allTeleporters = Map.FindMapObjectsByComponent("LinkedTeleporter");

        for (teleporter in allTeleporters)
        {
            teleporterComponent = teleporter.GetComponent("LinkedTeleporter");
            GroupLabel = teleporterComponent.GroupLabel;
            TargetGroupLabel = teleporterComponent.TargetGroupLabel;

            # Random sequence teleporters
            if (TargetGroupLabel == "RandomSequence")
            {
                if (GroupLabel == "Start")
                {
                    self._startTeleporters.Add(teleporter);
                }
                elif (GroupLabel == "End")
                {
                    self._endTeleporters.Add(teleporter);
                }
                elif (!self._randomSequenceLabels.Contains(GroupLabel))
                {
                    self._randomSequenceLabels.Add(GroupLabel);
                }
            }

            # General teleporters
            groupTeleporters = self._teleporterDict.Get(GroupLabel, List());
            groupTeleporters.Add(teleporter);
            self._teleporterDict.Set(GroupLabel, groupTeleporters);

            teleporterComponent._initialized = true;
        }
    }

    # Link all generic teleporters to their exit group.
    function InitTeleporters()
    {
        for (teleporterGroup in self._teleporterDict.Values)
        {
            for (teleporter in teleporterGroup)
            {
                teleporterComponent = teleporter.GetComponent("LinkedTeleporter");
                groupLabel = teleporterComponent.GroupLabel;
                targetGroupLabel = teleporterComponent.TargetGroupLabel;

                if (groupLabel != "RandomSequence" && self._teleporterDict.Contains(targetGroupLabel))
                {
                    exitTeleporters = self._teleporterDict.Get(targetGroupLabel);
                    self.LinkTeleporterToGroup(teleporter, exitTeleporters);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    # Link all teleporters in the random sequence.
    function InitRandomTeleporters()
    {
        if (self._randomSequenceLabels.Count == 0)
        {
            return;
        }

        # Create a random sequence of the teleporter groups starting with the "Start" group and ending with the "End" group
        randomGroupLabels = self._randomSequenceLabels;
        randomGroupLabels.Randomize();

        # Link intermediate teleporter groups
        for (index in Range(0, randomGroupLabels.Count - 1, 1))
        {
            entranceLabel = randomGroupLabels.Get(index);
            exitLabel = randomGroupLabels.Get(index + 1);
            teleporterEntrances = self._teleporterDict.Get(entranceLabel);
            teleporterExits = self._teleporterDict.Get(exitLabel);

            self.LinkTeleporterGroups(teleporterEntrances, teleporterExits);
        }

        # Link the start group to the first group in the random sequence
        firstGroup = self._teleporterDict.Get(randomGroupLabels.Get(0));
        self.LinkTeleporterGroups(self._startTeleporters, firstGroup);

        # Link the last group in the random sequence to the end group
        lastGroup = self._teleporterDict.Get(randomGroupLabels.Get(randomGroupLabels.Count - 1));
        self.LinkTeleporterGroups(lastGroup, self._endTeleporters);
    }

    # Set a single teleporter to target a random teleporter in an exit group
    function LinkTeleporterToGroup(teleporterEntrance, teleporterExits)
    {
        validExits = self.FilterValidExits(teleporterExits);

        if (validExits.Count > 0)
        {
            exitTeleporter = self.GetRandomFromList(validExits);
            teleporterEntrance.GetComponent("LinkedTeleporter").SetTeleportTarget(exitTeleporter);
        }
    }

    # Set a each teleporter in an entrance group to target a random teleporter in an exit group
    function LinkTeleporterGroups(teleporterEntrances, teleporterExits)
    {
        validExits = self.FilterValidExits(teleporterExits);

        if (validExits.Count > 0)
        {
            exitTeleporter = self.GetRandomFromList(validExits);
            exitTeleporter.GetComponent("LinkedTeleporter")._exitSet = true;

            for (entrance in teleporterEntrances)
            {
                entrance.GetComponent("LinkedTeleporter").SetTeleportTarget(exitTeleporter);
            }
        }
    }

    function FilterValidExits(teleporterList)
    {
        validExitList = List();

        for (teleporter in teleporterList)
        {
            if (!teleporter.GetComponent("LinkedTeleporter")._exitSet)
            {
                validExitList.Add(teleporter);
            }
        }

        return validExitList;
    }

    function GetRandomFromList(list)
    {
        randomIndex = Random.RandomInt(0, list.Count);
        return list.Get(randomIndex);
    }
}

component LinkedTeleporter
{
    # Inspector variables
    GroupLabel = "";
    TargetGroupLabel = "";
    NoEntrance = false;
    NoExit = false;
    ClearHooks = true;
    EnterAxis = "None";
    ExitAxis = "None";
    EnterAxisDropbox = "None,+x,-x,+y,-y,+z,-z";
    ExitAxisDropbox = "None,+x,-x,+y,-y,+z,-z";

    # Private variables
    _initialized = false;
    _targetPosition = null;
    _targetComponent = null;
    _enterVector = null;
    _exitVector = null;
    _exitset = false;
    _teleportCooldown = false; # Determine if a player was teleported to this teleporter and hasn't exited yet.

    # Inspector descriptions
    Description = "An advanced teleporter component that supports seamless transitions, teleporter grouping, and creating randomized sequences of teleporters.";
    GroupLabelTooltip = "A label to group one or more teleporters. Other teleporters can target this group by referencing GroupLabel. The 'Start' and 'End' labels are reserved for when TargetGroupLabel is set to 'RandomSequence'.";
    TargetGroupLabelTooltip = "The target group where the player should be teleported. When there are multiple teleporters in the target group, one is selected at random. When set to 'RandomSequence', this group is added to a random sequence beginning with the 'Start' group, visiting each 'RandomSequence' group, and ending with the 'End' group.";
    NoEntranceTooltip = "Prevent players from teleporting to this teleporter.";
    NoExitTooltip = "Prevent players from teleporting out of this teleporter.";
    ClearHooksTooltip = "When enabled, clear the player's hooks when teleported";
    EnterAxisTooltip = "The local axis of a teleporter from which a player is expected to enter. When EnterAxis and ExitAxis are provided for the entrance and exit teleporter, the player velocity and camera transition seamlessly.";
    ExitAxisTooltip = "The local axis of a teleporter from which a player will exit. When EnterAxis is not provided, the player velocity and camera are aligned to ExitAxis. When ExitAxis is not provided, the player velocity and camera don't change.";

    function OnGameStart()
    {
        if (self.GroupLabel == "End")
        {
            self.NoEntrance = true;
        }

        self._exitSet = self.NoExit;
        self._enterVector = self.ObjectAxisToVector(self.MapObject, self.EnterAxis);
        self._exitVector = self.ObjectAxisToVector(self.MapObject, self.ExitAxis);
    }

    function OnCollisionEnter(other)
    {
        if (self._initialized == false)
        {
            Game.Print("");
            Game.Print("Teleporter is not initialized! Make sure the Game Mode is set to 'Map Logic' and the teleporter is configured correctly.");
            return;
        }

        if (other.IsMainCharacter && other.Type == "Human" && !self.NoEntrance && !self._teleportCooldown)
        {
            self._targetComponent._teleportCooldown = true;
            self.TeleportPlayer(other);
        }
    }

    function OnCollisionExit(other)
    {
        self._teleportCooldown = false;
    }

    function SetTeleportTarget(targetTeleporter)
    {
        self._targetPosition = targetTeleporter.Position;
        self._targetComponent = targetTeleporter.GetComponent("LinkedTeleporter");
    }

    function TeleportPlayer(player)
    {
        self.ClearPlayerHooks(player);
        self.SetPlayerPosition(player);
        self.SetPlayerVelocity(player);
        self.SetPlayerCamera(player);
        self.SetCharacterLookDir(player);
    }

    function ClearPlayerHooks(player)
    {
        if (self.ClearHooks)
        {
            player.ClearHooks();
        }
    }

    function SetPlayerPosition(player)
    {
        TargetExitVector = self._targetComponent._exitVector;

        # Position the player to transition between the enter and exit axis.
        if (self._enterVector != null && TargetExitVector != null)
        {
            # Get offset between player and entrance teleporter.
            PositionOffset = self.MapObject.Position - player.Position;
            # Rotate the position offset to transition between the enter and exit axis.
            RotationOffset = Quaternion.FromToRotation(self._enterVector, TargetExitVector);
            PositionOffset = RotationOffset * PositionOffset;
            # Add the position offset to the target teleporter position.
            player.Position = self._targetPosition - PositionOffset;
        }
        # Center the player in the target teleporter.
        else
        {
            player.Position = self._targetPosition;
        }
    }

    function SetPlayerVelocity(player)
    {
        TargetExitVector = self._targetComponent._exitVector;

        # Rotate the player velocity to transition between the enter and exit axis.
        if (self._enterVector != null && TargetExitVector != null)
        {
            RotationOffset = Quaternion.FromToRotation(self._enterVector, TargetExitVector);
            player.Velocity = RotationOffset * player.Velocity;
        }
        # Align the player velocity with the exit axis.
        elif (TargetExitVector != null)
        {
            player.Velocity = TargetExitVector * player.Velocity.Magnitude;
        }
    }

    function SetPlayerCamera(player)
    {
        TargetExitVector = self._targetComponent._exitVector;

        # Rotate the player camera to transition between the enter and exit axis.
        if (self._enterVector != null && TargetExitVector != null)
        {
            AxisOffsetQuat = Quaternion.FromToRotation(self._enterVector, TargetExitVector);
            Camera.Forward = AxisOffsetQuat * Camera.Forward;
        }
        # Align the player camera with the exit axis.
        elif (TargetExitVector != null)
        {
            Camera.Forward = TargetExitVector;
        }
    }

    function SetCharacterLookDir(player)
    {
        TargetExitVector = self._targetComponent._exitVector;

        # Rotate the player character to transition between the enter and exit axis.
        if (self._enterVector != null && TargetExitVector != null)
        {
            AxisOffsetQuat = Quaternion.FromToRotation(self._enterVector, TargetExitVector);
            player.Forward = AxisOffsetQuat * player.Forward;
        }
        # Align the player character with the exit axis.
        elif (TargetExitVector != null)
        {
            player.Forward = TargetExitVector;
        }
    }

    function ObjectAxisToVector(mapObject, axisString)
    {
        if (axisString == "+x")
        {
            return mapObject.Right;
        }
        elif (axisString == "-x")
        {
            return mapObject.Right * -1;
        }
        elif (axisString == "+y")
        {
            return mapObject.Up;
        }
        elif (axisString == "-y")
        {
            return mapObject.Up * -1;
        }
        elif (axisString == "+z")
        {
            return mapObject.Forward;
        }
        elif (axisString == "-z")
        {
            return mapObject.Forward * -1;
        }
        else
        {
            return null;
        }
    }
}
